#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

/bin/bash -lc 'cat nbody/nbody.cpp' in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/nbody_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_omp_r2
 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

 void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
     const size_t n = bodies.size();
-    
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+    const size_t fullBlocks = n / FORCE_BLOCK;
+
+    // Called by every thread in the persistent simulation team. SIMD lanes
+    // handle different bodies, preserving each body's original summation order.
+    #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+    for (size_t block = 0; block < fullBlocks; ++block) {
+        const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK;
+        alignas(64) double x[FORCE_BLOCK], y[FORCE_BLOCK], z[FORCE_BLOCK];
+        alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            const double sourceX = bodies[j].pos.x;
+            const double sourceY = bodies[j].pos.y;
+            const double sourceZ = bodies[j].pos.z;
+
+            #pragma omp simd aligned(x, y, z, Fx, Fy, Fz : 64)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+                const double dx = sourceX - x[k];
+                const double dy = sourceY - y[k];
+                const double dz = sourceZ - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            bodies[first + k].vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+            bodies[first + k].vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+            bodies[first + k].vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+        }
+    }
+
+    // The final worksharing barrier also waits for all full blocks above.
+    #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = fullBlocks * FORCE_BLOCK; i < n; ++i) {
         double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
         
         for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {

+    #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+    for (size_t block = 0; block < fullBlocks; ++block) {
+        const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK;
+        alignas(64) double x[FORCE_BLOCK], y[FORCE_BLOCK], z[FORCE_BLOCK];
+        alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};

+            y[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.z;

+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;

+            }

+            bodies[first + k].vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+            bodies[first + k].vel.z += DT * Fz[k];

+    // handle different bodies, preserving each body's original summation order.
+    #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+    for (size_t block = 0; block < fullBlocks; ++block) {
+        const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK;
+        alignas(64) double x[FORCE_BLOCK], y[FORCE_BLOCK], z[FORCE_BLOCK];
+        alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+

+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.z;

+            const double sourceY = bodies[j].pos.y;
+            const double sourceZ = bodies[j].pos.z;
+

+                const double dx = sourceX - x[k];
+                const double dy = sourceY - y[k];
+                const double dz = sourceZ - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;

+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+

+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {

+            Fy[k] *= DT;
+            Fz[k] *= DT;
+        }

+    #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
+    for (size_t block = 0; block < fullBlocks; ++block) {
+        const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK;
+        alignas(64) double x[FORCE_BLOCK], y[FORCE_BLOCK], z[FORCE_BLOCK];
+        alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.z;
+        }

+            for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+                const double dx = sourceX - x[k];
+                const double dy = sourceY - y[k];

+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;

+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+

+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            Fx[k] *= DT;
+            Fy[k] *= DT;
+            Fz[k] *= DT;
+        }
+
+        #pragma omp simd

+            bodies[first + k].vel.y += Fy[k];
+            bodies[first + k].vel.z += Fz[k];
+        }
+    }
+

+    #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = fullBlocks * FORCE_BLOCK; i < n; ++i) {

    27	void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    28	    for (auto& body : bodies) {
    29	        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    30	        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    31	        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    32	        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    33	        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    34	        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    35	    }
    36	}

    97	void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    98	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    99	    #pragma omp for simd schedule(static)
   100	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   101	        auto& body = bodies[i];
   102	        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
   103	        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
   104	        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
   105	    }
   106	}
   107	
   108	double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   109	    double energy = 0.0;
   110	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
   111	    const int numThreads = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(
   112	        omp_get_max_threads(), std::max<size_t>(1, n)));
   113	
   114	    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(bodies, n) reduction(+:energy)
   115	    {
   116	        // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
   117	        #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait
   118	        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   119	            const auto& body = bodies[i];
   120	            energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +
   121	                            body.vel.y * body.vel.y +
   122	                            body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). Cyclic
   126	        // distribution balances the progressively shorter rows.
   127	        #pragma omp for schedule(static, 1) nowait
   128	        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   129	            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
   130	                const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
   131	                const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
   132	                const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
   133	                const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
   134	                energy -= 1.0 / dist;
   135	            }
   136	        }
   137	    }
   138	    
   139	    return energy;
   140	}
   141	
   142	// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
   143	bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   144	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   145	        // Check for NaN or Inf values
   146	        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
   147	            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
   148	            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
   149	            return false;
   150	        }
   151	        
   152	        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
   153	        const double maxPos = 1e6;
   154	        const double maxVel = 1e6;
   155	        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
   156	            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   157	            return false;
   158	        }
   159	        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
   160	            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   161	            return false;
   162	        }
   163	    }
   164	    return true;
   165	}

The body-state hashes now match the original across 15 test cases with 1–16 threads, and energy values agree within floating-point tolerance. For 8,192 bodies over 10 steps, simulation time fell from about 4.95 seconds to 44 milliseconds with 32 threads. I’ll check larger thread counts and the final CMake build next.

/bin/bash -lc "python3 -u - <<'PY'
import os, re, statistics, subprocess, tempfile
from pathlib import Path
source=Path('nbody/nbody.cpp').read_text()
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='nbody-tune-') as directory:
    binaries = {}
    for block in [4, 8, 16]:
        name=directory + '/block' + str(block)
        variant=source.replace('constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = 4;', 'constexpr size_t FORCE_BLOCK = ' + str(block) + ';')
        subprocess.run(['g++', '-std=c++20', '-O3', '-march=native', '-fno-math-errno', '-fopenmp', '-I', 'nbody', '-x', 'c++', '-', '-o', name], input=variant, text=True, check=True)
        binaries[block]=name
    for n, steps, threads_list in [(1024, 100, [1, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256]), (8192, 10, [1, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256])]:
        for block in [4, 8, 16]:
            for threads in threads_list:
                env=dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_PROC_BIND='true', OMP_PLACES='cores')
                times=[]
                for _ in range(3):
                    out=subprocess.check_output([binaries[block], '-n', str(n), '-s', str(steps)], text=True, env=env)
                    times.append(int(re.search(r'Simulation time: ([0-9]+)',out)[1]))
                print('bodies', n, 'steps', steps, 'block', block, 'threads', threads, 'milliseconds', times, flush=True)

 void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
     const size_t n = bodies.size();

-        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
-        
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK;
+
+    // Called by every thread in the persistent simulation team. SIMD lanes
+    // handle different bodies, preserving each body's original summation order.
+    #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+        const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK;
+        const size_t count = std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first);
+        // Pad the last block so the force loop always has a full SIMD width.
+        alignas(64) double x[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, y[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, z[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+        alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.z;
+        }
+

-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
-            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            const double sourceX = bodies[j].pos.x;
+            const double sourceY = bodies[j].pos.y;
+            const double sourceZ = bodies[j].pos.z;
+
+            #pragma omp simd aligned(x, y, z, Fx, Fy, Fz : 64)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+                const double dx = sourceX - x[k];
+                const double dy = sourceY - y[k];
+                const double dz = sourceZ - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // Scale separately to preserve rounding of the velocity increments.
+        #pragma omp simd aligned(Fx, Fy, Fz : 64)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            Fx[k] *= DT;
+            Fy[k] *= DT;
+            Fz[k] *= DT;
+        }
+
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+            bodies[first + k].vel.x += Fx[k];
+            bodies[first + k].vel.y += Fy[k];
+            bodies[first + k].vel.z += Fz[k];

    count = 0
    max_energy_difference = 0.0
    for n, steps in [(0, 0), (0, 2), (1, 10), (2, 10), (3, 3), (4, 3), (7, 3), (15, 10), (16, 10), (17, 10), (31, 5), (32, 5), (33, 5), (65, 5), (257, 10), (1024, 10), (1024, 100), (4096, 4), (8193, 2), (17, -1)]:

        expected = subprocess.check_output([reference, *args], text=True)
        expected_results = expected.split('=== RESULTS ===')[1].split('=== END RESULTS ===')[0]
        expected_energy = float(re.search(r'Final energy: (.*)', expected)[1])
        thread_counts=[1, 2, 4, 8, 16]
        if n == 1024 and steps == 10:
            thread_counts.extend([32, 64, 128, 256])
        for threads in thread_counts:
            env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_PROC_BIND='true', OMP_PLACES='cores')
            actual = subprocess.check_output([executable, *args], text=True, env=env)
            actual_results = actual.split('=== RESULTS ===')[1].split('=== END RESULTS ===')[0]
            assert actual_results == expected_results, ('state mismatch', n, steps, threads, actual_results, expected_results)
            actual_energy = float(re.search(r'Final energy: (.*)', actual)[1])
            assert math.isclose(actual_energy, expected_energy, rel_tol=1e-10, abs_tol=1e-6), ('energy mismatch', n, steps, threads, actual_energy, expected_energy)
            assert 'Validation: PASSED' in actual
            max_energy_difference=max(max_energy_difference, abs(actual_energy-expected_energy))
            count+=1
        print('Exact state match:', n, 'bodies,', steps, 'steps, threads=' + '/'.join(map(str, thread_counts)), flush=True)
    print('PASS:', count, 'comparisons; maximum absolute energy difference', max_energy_difference, flush=True)
    for args in [['-h'], ['--unknown'], ['-n'], ['-s']]:
        expected = subprocess.run([reference, *args], capture_output=True, text=True)
        actual = subprocess.run([executable, *args], capture_output=True, text=True)
        assert actual.returncode == expected.returncode
        assert actual.stdout.replace(executable, 'PROGRAM') == expected.stdout.replace(reference, 'PROGRAM')
    print('PASS: help and error handling', flush=True)

A group size of 16 performs well and limits excess workers on small inputs. The 8,192-body benchmark reached about 26 milliseconds with 64 threads. I’m checking the final version against the original, including uneven body counts, longer runs, and sanitizer checks.

   176	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   177	    int numBodies = 1024;
   178	    int numSteps = 10;
   179	    bool validate = false;
   180	    bool printResults = false;
   181	    
   182	    // Parse command line arguments
   183	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   184	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   185	            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
   186	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   187	            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
   188	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   189	            validate = true;
   190	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   191	            printResults = true;
   192	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   193	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   194	            return 0;
   195	        } else {
   196	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   197	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   198	            return 1;
   199	        }
   200	    }
   201	    
   202	    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
   203	    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
   204	    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
   205	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   206	    
   207	    // Initialize bodies
   208	    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
   209	    randomizeBodies(bodies);
   210	    
   211	    // Run simulation
   212	    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   213	    
   214	    // Avoid creating more workers than there are force blocks to process.
   215	    const int numThreads = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(),
   216	        std::max(1, static_cast<int>((bodies.size() + FORCE_BLOCK - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK)));
   217	    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(bodies, numSteps)
   218	    {
   219	        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
   220	            computeForces(bodies);
   221	            integrateBodies(bodies);
   222	        }
   223	    }
   224	    
   225	    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   226	    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
   227	    
   228	    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
   229	    
   230	    // Print results for external validation
   231	    if (printResults) {
   232	        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
   233	        std::vector<double> bodyData;
   234	        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
   235	        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   236	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
   237	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
   238	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
   239	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
   240	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
   241	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
   242	        }
   243	        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
   244	    }
   245	    
   246	    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
   247	    if (validate) {
   248	        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
   249	        
   250	        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
   251	            // Report final energy for reference
   252	            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
   253	            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
   254	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   255	            return 0;
   256	        } else {
   257	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   258	            return 1;
   259	        }
   260	    }
   261	    
   262	    return 0;
   263	}

 void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
     const size_t n = bodies.size();
-    
+    const size_t numBlocks = (n + FORCE_BLOCK - 1) / FORCE_BLOCK;
+
+    // Called by every thread in the persistent simulation team. SIMD lanes
+    // handle different bodies, preserving each body's original summation order.
+    #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+        const size_t first = block * FORCE_BLOCK;
+        const size_t count = std::min(FORCE_BLOCK, n - first);
+        // Pad the last block so the force loop always has a full SIMD width.
+        alignas(64) double x[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, y[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, z[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+        alignas(64) double Fx[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fy[FORCE_BLOCK] = {}, Fz[FORCE_BLOCK] = {};
+
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[first + k].pos.z;
+        }
+
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            const double sourceX = bodies[j].pos.x;
+            const double sourceY = bodies[j].pos.y;
+            const double sourceZ = bodies[j].pos.z;
+
+            #pragma omp simd aligned(x, y, z, Fx, Fy, Fz : 64)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+                const double dx = sourceX - x[k];
+                const double dy = sourceY - y[k];
+                const double dz = sourceZ - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // Scale separately to preserve rounding of the velocity increments.
+        #pragma omp simd aligned(Fx, Fy, Fz : 64)
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_BLOCK; ++k) {
+            Fx[k] *= DT;
+            Fy[k] *= DT;
+            Fz[k] *= DT;
+        }
+
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < count; ++k) {
+            bodies[first + k].vel.x += Fx[k];
+            bodies[first + k].vel.y += Fy[k];
+            bodies[first + k].vel.z += Fz[k];
+        }
+    }

codex
Implemented OpenMP with a persistent thread team, SIMD force blocks, parallel integration, and energy reduction. CMake requires OpenMP; the executable remains `nbody`. Only the two existing files in `nbody/` changed.

Verified 104 comparisons: body-state hashes matched exactly, energy agreed within floating-point tolerance, and sanitizer checks passed.

For 8,192 bodies over 10 steps: **4.95 s → 26 ms** with 64 bound threads, about **190× faster** on this CPU.

Implemented OpenMP with a persistent thread team, SIMD force blocks, parallel integration, and energy reduction. CMake requires OpenMP; the executable remains `nbody`. Only the two existing files in `nbody/` changed.

Verified 104 comparisons: body-state hashes matched exactly, energy agreed within floating-point tolerance, and sanitizer checks passed.

For 8,192 bodies over 10 steps: **4.95 s → 26 ms** with 64 bound threads, about **190× faster** on this CPU.
